# Pokémon

Generated from the data files by `scripts/build_reference_docs.py`. Don't edit by hand.

Covers the Redux roster: all 493 Pokémon of Gens 1–4 plus the later evolutions it adds. "Changed" means different from hg-engine, not from the original HeartGold, so changes hg-engine already made aren't flagged.

TMs/HMs and tutor moves list only what the game teaches. Friendship evolutions need 80 friendship in every case.

## Changes from hg-engine

This table covers types, abilities, stats and evolutions. Each Pokémon's entry below lists these along with its move and egg cycle changes.

| Pokémon | Change |
|---|---|
| Primeape | New evolution: Level 36 → Annihilape<br>Removed evolution: Form Argument (20) → Annihilape |
| Poliwhirl | New evolution: Use King’s Rock → Politoed |
| Kadabra | New evolution: Level 36 → Alakazam |
| Machoke | New evolution: Level 36 → Machamp |
| Graveler | New evolution: Level 36 → Golem |
| Slowpoke | New evolution: Use King’s Rock → Slowking |
| Haunter | New evolution: Level 36 → Gengar |

| Scyther | New evolution: Use Metal Coat → Scizor |
| Electabuzz | New evolution: Use Electirizer → Electivire |
| Magmar | New evolution: Use Magmarizer → Magmortar |
| Porygon | New evolution: Use Upgrade → Porygon2 |
| Meganium | Type: Grass → Grass/Fairy |
| Feraligatr | Type: Water → Water/Dragon |
| Ampharos | Type: Electric → Electric/Dragon |
| Gligar | New evolution: Use Razor Fang → Gliscor |
